*Zhiyan Hou,Dan Zhang,Tao Feng,Liyuan Wang,Wei Li,Xiangzhao Hao,Hongyan An,Junfeng Fang,Haokai Ma,Zhaohui Xu,Haiyun Guo,Jinqiao Wang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从参数中心学习向系统级适应的转变视角出发，提出一个三维分析框架（When/How/Where），系统综述了持续学习领域从经典参数中心机制到新兴范式（如on-policy学习、测试时训练、外部结构组件）的演化，并讨论了该范式转变带来的挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的持续学习主要聚焦于参数中心机制（训练策略、架构设计、权重适应），但新兴的发展——如on-policy学习、测试时训练、记忆库/技能库/交互协议等外部组件——正在将持续学习的边界拓展到远超静态参数空间的范围。这一转变意味着持续学习正从参数中心学习走向系统级适应，但缺乏一个统一的分析框架来刻画这一演化过程。

Method: 提出一个三维分析框架来刻画持续学习的演化：（1）How（如何学习）——涵盖off-policy、on-policy及超越梯度的优化机制；（2）When（何时学习）——涵盖预训练、后训练和推理阶段三个时期的演化；（3）Where（何处学习）——区分内部参数更新与外部结构约束。基于这一三轴框架，系统梳理代表性方法并追溯持续学习的转型轨迹。

Result: 通过该三维框架系统调查了持续学习的代表性方法，刻画了持续学习从传统参数中心机制向系统级适应（涉及外部记忆、技能库、交互协议等组件）的过渡过程，并识别出这一范式转变所带来的关键挑战。

Conclusion: 持续学习正经历从参数中心学习到系统级适应的根本性转变，三维框架（When/How/Where）为理解这一转变提供了统一的视角；该转变对持续学习的理论框架、方法设计和应用实践均具有深远影响，未来需要在该框架下进一步探索新的研究方向。

Abstract: Classical continual learning (CL) has primarily focused on enabling models to update and retain knowledge through parameter-centric mechanisms, e.g., training strategies, architectural designs, and weight adaptation. However, emerging paradigms are reshaping the scope of CL beyond this traditional model adaptation view. For instance, on-policy learning broadens the space of update mechanisms; test-time training extends CL from the training phase to inference; and external harness components such as memory, skill libraries, and interaction protocols extend the evolutionary boundaries of model capabilities far beyond the static parameter space. Collectively, these developments indicate a transition from parameter-centric learning toward system-level adaptation. To characterize this transition, we examine the evolution of continual learning through three dimensions: When, How, and Where learning occurs. The How dimension encompasses off-policy, on-policy, and beyond-gradient optimization mechanics. The When dimension captures evolution across pre-training, post-training, and inference-time stages. The Where dimension delineates updates occurring within internal parameters versus external structural constraints. Anchored by this tri-axial framework, we systematically survey representative methods, trace the ongoing transition of continual learning, and discuss the key challenges, broader implications, and future directions arising from this paradigm shift.

</details>


### [63] [Timestep-Conditioned Transformers for Global Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06241)
*Sam Levang,Fran Bartolic,Ty Dickinson,Chase Dwelle,Paulius Rauba,Viktor Cikojevic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GEM-3 是一个概率性全球天气预报模型，通过显式多时间步推理（multi-timestep inference）解决固定自回归时间步带来的权衡问题。使用单组训练权重，可在推理时配置时间步长以平衡可预测性与实用性，并采用混合时间步训练提升滚动稳定性。该模型基于约1.34亿参数的轻量级邻域注意力Transformer，在中期概率预报、扩展范围滚动、训练推理效率和决策相关诊断方面均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习天气预报模型依赖预定且固定的自回归时间步，而时间步的选择存在根本性权衡：短时间步（1-6小时）能精细解析昼夜循环内的大气动力学，但会加剧给定预报时域上的误差累积；长时间步（24小时）虽减少误差累积，却限制了次日常可预测性高的短期预报的实用性。作者旨在解决这一权衡问题。

Method: GEM-3 采用显式多时间步推理机制，通过单组训练权重在推理阶段灵活配置模型时间步长，从而在广泛的预报时域上平衡可预测性与实用性。此外提出混合时间步训练策略，相较于时间步专业化的模型，能持续改善滚动稳定性。模型架构为轻量级邻域注意力Transformer（约134M参数），基于等距柱状网格，并在其前身GEM-2基础上引入了多项架构改进。

Result: GEM-3 实现了接近最先进的（near-SOTA）中期概率预报技能，具备稳定的扩展范围滚动预报能力，同时保持高效的训练与推理效率，并能提供决策相关的诊断信息，构成一个实用的天气预报系统。

Conclusion: GEM-3 通过多时间步推理和混合时间步训练，有效化解了固定时间步的权衡难题，在准确性、稳定性与实用性之间取得了良好平衡，为机器学习天气预报提供了一个兼具高性能和高效率的实用方案。

Abstract: Existing machine-learning weather forecasting models rely on predetermined and fixed autoregressive timesteps. The choice of model timestep involves a fundamental trade-off: shorter timesteps (e.g. 1 to 6 hours) finely resolve atmospheric dynamics within the diurnal cycle but increase error accumulation for a given forecast horizon, while longer timesteps (e.g. 24 hours) reduce error accumulation but limit the usability of short-range forecasts where sub-daily predictability is high. In this work, we present GEM-3, a probabilistic global weather model that addresses this trade-off through explicit multi-timestep inference. With a single set of trained weights, the model timestep can be configured at inference time to balance predictability and usability across a broad forecast horizon. Additionally, we find that mixed-timestep training consistently improves rollout stability relative to timestep-specialist models. Under the hood, GEM-3 is a lightweight neighborhood-attention transformer with ~134M parameters on an equirectangular grid with a number of architectural advancements beyond its predecessor GEM-2. The result is a practical forecasting system that couples near-SOTA medium-range probabilistic skill, stable extended-range rollouts, efficient training and inference, and decision-relevant diagnostics.

</details>


### [64] [A Six-Dimensional Taxonomy of Post-Training Adaptation Techniques with Applications in AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06246)
*Fardin Afdideh,Fernando Seoane,Farhad Abtahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文献综述系统梳理了后训练适应（post-training adaptation）的文献，提出了一个六维分类体系（机制、目标、数据需求、持久性、结构范围、模型类型），用于区分微调、检索增强、提示等常被混淆的术语，并映射了各技术之间的关系与演化路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练适应已成为现代机器学习实践的核心，涵盖重训练、微调、参数高效适应、对齐、检索增强、模型编辑、反学习、校准和多模态指令微调等多种技术。然而这些文献分散在不同技术族、模型类别和部署场景中，缺乏统一的比较方法和描述框架，导致难以对已训练模型的修改进行系统描述。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，系统整合后训练适应相关文献，提出了一个六维分类体系，包括机制（mechanism）、目标（goal）、数据需求（data requirement）、持久性（persistence）、结构范围（structural scope）和模型类型（model type）。此外，还建立了技术间的关系映射，包括继承（inheritance）、取代（supersession）、混合（hybridization）和分层部署栈（layered deployment stacks）。

Result: 该分类体系有效区分了微调、检索增强和提示等常被混用的术语，展示了适应策略如何从传统机器学习逐步演化到深度学习、基础模型、大语言模型和多模态大语言模型时代，并提供了支持技术文档、模型变更追踪和治理分析的统一词汇表。

Conclusion: 综述指出了后训练适应领域面临的开放挑战，包括评估、可复现性、持久性推理时适应、反学习、多模态适应以及面向治理的后训练工作流等问题，为未来研究方向提供了指引。

Abstract: Post-training adaptation has become central to modern machine learning practice and includes techniques such as retraining, fine-tuning, parameter-efficient adaptation, alignment, retrieval augmentation, model editing, unlearning, calibration, and Multimodal Instruction Tuning. However, the literature remains fragmented across technique families, model classes, and deployment contexts, making it difficult to compare methods or describe how a trained model has been modified. This survey synthesizes the post-training adaptation literature and introduces a six-dimensional taxonomy organized by mechanism, goal, data requirement, persistence, structural scope, and model type. The taxonomy distinguishes commonly conflated terms such as fine-tuning, retrieval augmentation, and prompting, and shows how adaptation strategies evolve from traditional machine learning through deep learning, foundation models, large language models, and multimodal large language models. It also maps relationships among techniques, including inheritance, supersession, hybridization, and layered deployment stacks. The resulting vocabulary can support technical documentation, model-change tracking, and governance analysis. The survey concludes by identifying open challenges in evaluation, reproducibility, persistent inference-time adaptation, unlearning, multimodal adaptation, and governance-aware post-training workflows.

</details>


### [65] [MetaboLLM: a metabolomics-specialized large language model for biochemical knowledge integration and predictive metabolite graph construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06253)
*Dohyun Ku,Min Gu Kwak,Francisco J. Pasquel,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MetaboLLM——一个面向代谢组学的专科大语言模型，通过持续预训练、监督微调和结构化检索适应代谢组学领域。同时提出MetaboLLM-GIN，将生成的生化描述转换为代谢物图，并利用图同构网络进行患者级预测。实验表明MetaboLLM在代谢组学知识、关系和描述任务上优于基础模型和医学适应模型，MetaboLLM-GIN在应激性高血糖预测（AUC 0.8616）和绝经后激素方案分类（AUC 0.8123）中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代谢组学知识分散在异构资源中，难以转化为可预测的表示形式，现有通用或医学领域大语言模型缺乏针对代谢组学的专业知识，亟需领域特化模型来组织和利用这些分散的生化知识。

Method: 提出两阶段方案：(1) MetaboLLM——通过持续预训练、监督微调和结构化检索对四种大语言模型骨干进行代谢组学领域适应；(2) MetaboLLM-GIN——将LLM生成的生化描述转换为代谢物图，并用图同构网络（GIN）进行患者级预测任务。

Result: MetaboLLM在四种骨干家族上均优于对应基础模型和医学适应模型，并能迁移到外部公共基准。MetaboLLM-GIN在冠状动脉搭桥术后应激性高血糖预测中取得最高AUC（0.8616），在绝经后激素方案分类中取得AUC 0.8123，优于传统模型、替代图构建方法及未适应或非检索LLM配置生成的图。

Conclusion: 领域特化语言模型能够将异构生化知识组织为可预测且可解释的代谢物图表示，这为代谢组学驱动的临床预测提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Metabolomics knowledge is distributed across heterogeneous resources and remains difficult to translate into predictive representations. We developed MetaboLLM, a metabolomics-specialized large language model adapted through continual pretraining, supervised fine-tuning, and structured retrieval, together with MetaboLLM-GIN, which converts generated biochemical descriptions into metabolite graphs for patient-level prediction using a graph isomorphism network. Across four backbone families, MetaboLLM outperformed corresponding base and medically adapted models on metabolomics knowledge, relational, and description tasks, and transferred to an external public benchmark. MetaboLLM-GIN achieved the highest AUC for stress hyperglycemia prediction after coronary artery bypass grafting (0.8616) and postmenopausal hormone-regimen classification (0.8123), outperforming conventional models, alternative graph constructions, and graphs generated from unadapted or non-retrieval LLM configurations. Model interpretation further produced biologically meaningful findings in both applications. These results show that domain-specialized language models can organize heterogeneous biochemical knowledge into predictive and interpretable metabolite graph representations.

</details>


### [66] [RxnCLF: Contrastive Transformation-Aware Reaction Foundation Model for Improved Reactivity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06259)
*Yiting Zheng,Cheng Fang,Anthony Donofrio,Haote Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RxnCLF，一种基于自监督对比学习的反应表征学习框架。它利用凝聚反应图（CRG）统一反应物和产物信息，在170万Pistachio反应上预训练，学习捕获反应中心和侧链上下文的紧凑连续潜在空间。在下游多个产率预测基准上（包括Buchwald-Hartwig、Pd催化偶联及专有的HTE C-N偶联和酰胺形成数据集）持续优于图与序列基线，揭示其作为可扩展反应基础模型的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应产率预测面临标记数据稀缺、反应空间组合爆炸且稀疏分布的问题。现有的字符串、指纹和图基反应编码仅能部分捕捉化学转化信息，对复杂底物反应难以准确预测，亟需更好的反应表征方法来提升泛化能力。

Method: RxnCLF采用自监督对比学习框架，核心创新是构建凝聚反应图（CRG），将反应物和产物信息统一为单一图结构，使模型学习显式且丰富的转化结构而非孤立图。模型在170万Pistachio反应上预训练，学习包含反应中心特征和侧链上下文的连续潜在空间，随后在下游产率预测任务上进行微调。

Result: 在多个产率预测基准（Buchwald-Hartwig、Pd催化BH偶联、专有HTE C-N偶联及酰胺形成数据集）上，RxnCLF持续优于图与序列基线，R2指标有所提升并获得最佳综合表现。

Conclusion: 基于CRG的RxnCLF展示了作为可扩展反应基础模型的潜力，有望在更广阔的反应空间中泛化，并支持区域选择性预测、对映选择性预测和反应条件优化等多样化的下游反应信息学任务。

Abstract: Reaction yield prediction remains challenging because labeled data are scarce and reaction space is both combinatorially large and sparsely populated, limiting the generalization of existing reaction representations. String-, fingerprint-, and graph-based reaction encodings only partially capture chemical transformations, making accurate prediction difficult for reactions with complex substrates. We propose reaction contrastive learning foundation (RxnCLF), a self-supervised contrastive framework for reaction representation learning. RxnCLF is built on a condensed reaction graph (CRG) that unifies reactant and product information into a single graph, enabling the model to learn explicit and enriched transformation structure rather than disconnected graphs. Pretrained on 1.7 million Pistachio reactions, RxnCLF learns a compact and continuous latent space that captures both reaction-center features and broader side chain contexts, making it transformation-aware and chemically interpretable. Fine-tuned on multiple yield prediction benchmarks, including Buchwald-Hartwig, Pd-catalyzed BH coupling, and proprietary HTE C-N coupling and amide formation datasets, RxnCLF consistently outperforms graph and sequence-based baselines, improving R2 and achieving the best performance overall. Our results highlight the promise of CRG-based RxnCLF as a scalable reaction foundation model, with the potential to generalize across broader reaction spaces and support diverse downstream reaction informatics tasks, including regioselectivity prediction, enantioselectivity prediction, and reaction condition optimization.

</details>


### [67] [Hypothesis Testing with Conditional Queries: Learnability and the Value of Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06262)
*Zonghuan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究模型评估中自适应与非自适应查询之间的权衡。研究表明，在有限结果空间上，两个分布类可区分当且仅当它们的条件概率存在正分离；当分离为零时，任意有限查询预算下的最坏情况误差恰为1/2。对于任意T-查询自适应策略，可构造一个仅需O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))次非自适应成对查询的随机程序，其模拟转录与自适应转录在总变差距离内不超过ρ。文章进一步证明了最坏情况下自适应与非自适应查询复杂度之间的差距为Θ_ε(N²)，表明交互可以带来二次因子（quadratic factor）的查询数量减少，但不会产生指数级优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型评估中，评估者可以选择预先固定所有测试或根据早期响应选择后续测试。作者希望系统性地研究这两种策略之间的权衡，特别关注自适应测试器相对于非自适应测试器所需查询数量的理论差距，以及这种差距是否会随交互分支增长呈指数级扩大。

Method: 作者在条件查询模型（conditional query model）中，对有限结果空间（|X|=N）上的分布类进行研究。他们首先建立了可学习性（learnability）与两类分布间条件概率分离（separation）的充要条件。随后构建了一种随机非自适应程序，使用O(N²(T + log(1/ρ)))次成对查询（pair queries）来模拟任何T-查询自适应策略，并证明模拟产生的转录在总变差距离内与自适应转录ρ-接近。同时还构造了匹配的下界族，其自适应查询复杂度为常数而自适应差距为Ω_ε(N²)。

Result: 证明了可区分性条件：分布类可学习当且仅当条件概率存在正分离；当分离为零时，最优最坏误差恰为1/2。构造了上界O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))的非自适应模拟方案，并构造了匹配下界，最终证明最坏情况固定误差自适应差距为Θ_ε(N²)。

Conclusion: 交互（adaptive interaction）可以将所需测试次数减少一个二次因子（quadratic factor），但交互评估看似指数级的分支并不会带来指数级的查询优势。这一结论为模型评估设计中自适应与非自适应策略的选择提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Model evaluations may fix all tests before observing any responses or select later tests using earlier responses. We study this choice in a conditional-query model on a finite outcome space $\mathcal{X}$ with $|\mathcal{X}|=N$. We first ask which pairs of distribution classes can be reliably distinguished. We then ask how many additional queries are required to match an adaptive tester when all queried events must be fixed in advance. We show that learnability holds if and only if the two classes have positive separation in their pairwise conditional probabilities. When this separation is zero, the optimal worst-case error is exactly $1/2$ at every finite query budget. For any $T$-query adaptive policy and any $ρ\in (0,1)$, we construct a randomized non-adaptive procedure using $O(N^2(T + \log(1/ρ)))$ pair queries chosen before any response is observed. Its simulated transcript is within $ρ$ in total variation of the adaptive transcript, uniformly over all distributions in the model. We also construct a matching family with constant adaptive query complexity and $Ω_\varepsilon(N^2)$ non-adaptive query complexity. Consequently, the worst-case fixed-error adaptivity gap is $Θ_\varepsilon(N^2)$. Thus interaction can reduce the required number of tests by a quadratic factor, but the apparent exponential branching of an interactive evaluation does not yield an exponential query advantage.

</details>


### [68] [The Tamed Subgradient Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm beyond Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06283)
*Iosif Lytras,Nikolaos Makras,Sotirios Sabanis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SG-TULA算法，一种直接基于次梯度操作的非光滑、超线性梯度增长、非凸目标分布的采样方法，通过taming技术实现稳定显式离散化，并给出Wasserstein-2距离下的非渐近收敛界及优化问题的超额风险估计。作者还验证了该算法在GPT-2系列LLM正则化预训练势函数上的适用性，并证明其boosted坐标版本可与finetuned AdamW和Muon竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于次梯度的Langevin采样算法在处理同时满足非光滑、超线性梯度增长和非凸特性的目标分布时，面临收敛速率不理想或依赖计算昂贵的平滑化预处理的问题。需要一种直接基于次梯度、无需平滑化且具有更好非渐近收敛保证的算法。

Method: 提出Subgradient Tamed Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm (SG-TULA)，对Langevin扩散进行离散化，直接在次梯度上操作以规避平滑化过程；采用taming技术处理超线性梯度增长，保证方案的显式稳定性。推导了Wasserstein-2距离下的非渐近收敛界，所有常数均显式追踪维度与逆温度依赖性。

Result: 所得收敛速率优于目前已知的基于次梯度的Langevin算法；提供了相应的优化问题超额风险估计；验证了假设在GPT-2系列LLM正则化预训练势函数上成立（含显式常数）；boosted坐标变体SG-TULA在预训练性能上可与finetuned AdamW和Muon竞争，而这些方法目前缺乏可比性的非渐近保证。

Conclusion: SG-TULA为处理非光滑、超线性、非凸势函数的Langevin采样提供了理论上更优且实用的方案，不仅在理论上改进了收敛界，还在大规模LLM预训练场景中展现出实证竞争力，填补了此类问题缺乏严格非渐近保证的空白。

Abstract: We study the problem of sampling from target distributions whose potentials are simultaneously non-smooth, subject to superlinear gradient growth, and non-convex. We introduce the Subgradient Tamed Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm (SG-TULA), a discretisation of the Langevin diffusion that operates directly on subgradients, without relying on computationally demanding smoothing procedures. To handle the superlinear regime, taming techniques are employed to produce a stable, explicit scheme. We derive non-asymptotic convergence bounds in Wasserstein-2 distance, with all constants tracked explicitly in terms of dimension and inverse temperature, improving upon the currently known rates for subgradient-based Langevin algorithms. We further provide excess risk estimates for the associated optimisation problem. We verify the assumptions, with explicit constants, for the regularized pretraining potential of a LLM in the GPT-2 lineage and the boosted coordinate-wise variant of SG-TULA pretrains the former competitively against finetuned AdamW and Muon, for which no comparable non-asymptotic guarantees are presently available.

</details>


### [69] [Surv-IPTB: An Attention-Based Model for Estimating Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit with Survival Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06288)
*Lev V. Utkin,Stanislav K. Kogan,Andrei V. Konstantinov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Surv-IPTB，一种基于注意力机制的框架，用于估计生存分析中的个体治疗获益概率（IPTB）。该框架将IPTB估计重构为二元分类问题，利用治疗组和对照组之间的成对患者比较，通过不精确概率表示处理右删失观测，并使用可学习的查询-键变换注意力机制实现灵活的成对比较聚合。在包含螺旋、钟形和圆形特征空间的非线性合成数据集上的实验表明，该方法在不同删失率和治疗效果强度下均优于T-learner和S-learner等元学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生存分析中，估计个体治疗获益概率（IPTB）对于个性化医疗决策至关重要，但现有方法在处理非线性特征空间、右删失数据和复杂的治疗效应异质性时面临显著挑战。传统元学习基线（如T-learner、S-learner）在非线性场景下性能退化严重，需要一种更具表达能力和统计原则性的框架来解决这一问题。

Method: Surv-IPTB框架的核心方法包括：(1) 将IPTB估计重构为二元分类问题，通过治疗组与对照组之间的成对患者比较实现；(2) 利用不精确概率表示（区间值概率）原则性地处理右删失观测，对不确定的治疗效应进行表征；(3) 采用带有可学习查询-键变换的注意力机制，实现数据驱动的成对比较灵活聚合，同时为删失案例学习软类别概率。

Result: 在具有复杂非线性结构的合成数据集（螺旋、钟形和圆形特征空间）上的大量实验表明，Surv-IPTB在不同删失率和治疗效应强度下均保持稳健性能。该模型持续优于配备随机生存森林、Cox比例风险和Beran估计器的T-learner和S-learner元学习基线，尤其在传统方法显著退化的非线性挑战场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于注意力的框架被证明是一种可扩展且统计上原则性的解决方案，适用于生存背景下个性化治疗获益评估。模型的代码已公开，便于复现和应用推广。

Abstract: This work presents a novel attention-based framework for estimating the Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit (IPTB) in survival analysis contexts. The proposed model, called Surv-IPTB, directly quantifies the probability that a specific patient will experience extended survival time under treatment versus control. We reformulate IPTB estimation as a binary classification problem, leveraging pairwise patient comparisons across treatment and control cohorts. The framework incorporates a principled handling of right-censored observations through imprecise probability representations, where uncertain treatment effects are characterized by interval-valued probabilities. An attention mechanism with learnable query-key transformations enables flexible, data-driven aggregation of pairwise comparisons, while simultaneously learning soft class probabilities for censored cases. Through extensive experiments on synthetic datasets with complex nonlinear structures, including spiral, bell-shaped, and circular feature spaces, we demonstrate that our approach maintains robust performance across varying censoring rates and treatment effect strengths. The model consistently outperforms meta-learner baselines (T-learner and S-learner) equipped with random survival forests, Cox proportional hazards, and Beran estimators, particularly in challenging nonlinear scenarios where conventional methods exhibit significant degradation. The results establish the proposed attention-based framework as a scalable and statistically principled solution for personalized treatment benefit assessment in survival settings. The code implementing the model is publicly available.

</details>


### [70] [BaKron: Efficient Quantization with Kronecker-Factored Hessians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06291)
*Johann Birnick,Rayan Saab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BaKron是一种高效的两侧自适应舍入求解器，利用Kronecker分解的Hessian近似信息进行神经网络量化，通过反对角并行和递归分治策略将总计算复杂度从O(m²n²)降至O(mn(m+n))，匹配GPTQ的三次方缩放同时利用更丰富的曲率信息，且与基础量化器和Hessian估计器均保持模块化兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPTQ风格的自适应舍入仅使用来自输入激活的单侧信息，无法捕获输出坐标间的相关性。虽然两侧Kronecker分解的Hessian近似可以捕获这些相关性，但直接在向量化权重域应用GPTQ计算代价过高。本文旨在构建一个高效的两侧自适应舍入求解器，在利用丰富曲率信息的同时保持与GPTQ相当的计算效率。

Method: 本文基于BoA和YAQA的两侧自适应舍入公式，提出BaKron求解器，其核心创新包括：(1) 反对角线并行策略，有效利用Kronecker分解结构的稀疏性；(2) 递归分治构造过程，实现层次化的计算复用。对于m×n权重矩阵，BaKron将顺序步数控制在O(m+n)，同时将总工作量从O(m²n²)降至O(mn(m+n))。

Result: BaKron实现了与GPTQ相同的立方级（三次方）计算缩放，但利用了更丰富的Hessian曲率信息。论文提供了实际基准测试，考察了BaKron可配用的多种Hessian估计器，找到了一种高效计算这些Hessian的技术，并进行了实验验证。

Conclusion: BaKron在计算效率和模型质量之间取得了良好平衡，既保留了Kronecker分解Hessian提供的两侧曲率信息带来的量化精度优势，又通过算法层面的优化将计算成本降至与GPTQ相同的量级，且具有模块化设计，可灵活搭配不同的基础量化器和Hessian估计器。

Abstract: We accelerate a family of algorithms for neural network quantization whose geometry is informed by any Kronecker-factored approximation of the Hessian. GPTQ-style adaptive rounding typically uses one-sided information derived from input activations. Two-sided Kronecker-factored Hessian approximations can additionally capture correlations across output coordinates, but applying GPTQ directly in the vectorized weight domain is computationally expensive. Building on the two-sided adaptive-rounding formulation used by BoA and YAQA, we introduce BaKron, an efficient solver that combines anti-diagonal parallelism with a recursive divide-and-conquer construction. For an $m\times n$ weight matrix, BaKron uses $O(m+n)$ sequential steps while reducing the total work from $O(m^2n^2)$ to $O(mn(m+n))$. Thus, it matches the cubic scaling of GPTQ while exploiting richer curvature information. Moreover, BaKron is modular with respect to both the base quantizer and the Hessian estimator. We also provide practical benchmarks, consider a range of Hessians that BaKron can be called with, find an efficient technique to compute these Hessians, and evaluate the algorithm experimentally.

</details>


### [71] [On-Policy Self-Distillation without Any Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06296)
*Yijiang Li,Bingyang Wang,Yijun Liang,Yunjie Tian,Di Fu,Nuno Vasconcelos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出U-OPSD（无监督在线策略自蒸馏）方法，仅利用模型自身生成结果通过内部一致性实现真正的自蒸馏，无需任何外部监督信号（如真值标签、环境反馈或更大模型指导），在多个数学推理基准上匹配或超越依赖真值的有监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线策略（自）蒸馏方法（OPD/OPSD）虽然展现了大语言模型后训练的巨大潜力，但依然严重依赖外部监督信号（真值、环境反馈或更大模型的指导），无法实现真正的"自"蒸馏。作者旨在探索仅利用模型自身生成内容即可完成自蒸馏的可能性。

Method: U-OPSD首先采样多条rollout（多次生成），通过多数投票与自一致性阈值构造伪解决方案；然后以最短伪解决方案为条件构建教师分布，将其蒸馏到模型最长错误补全的前缀中，使模型能精准地在其高置信度出错的位置进行自我纠正。整个过程无任何外部监督信息参与。

Result: 在AIME24、AIME25、HMMT25、MATH500和AMC23等多个基准上，U-OPSD相较于基础模型在Qwen3非思考模式下4B和8B规模上分别提升8.5%和10.7%，平均超越OPSD 3.2%和2.3%；在思考模式下，4B规模超越OPSD 0.9%、8B规模与OPSD持平，分别超越GRPO 0.7%和1.1%。

Conclusion: 实验证明，仅利用模型自身生成结果和内部一致性即可实现有效的在线策略自蒸馏，无需任何外部监督。U-OPSD在不同基准、基础模型和训练设置下均稳定提升，且匹配或超越依赖真值的有监督方法（OPSD、GRPO），表明自我纠错式的自蒸馏范式具有广阔前景。

Abstract: On-policy (Self-)Distillation (OPD / OPSD) has shown strong potential for post-training large language models (LLMs). However, existing methods still rely heavily on external supervision, including ground-truth signals, environmental feedback, or guidance from larger models, and therefore fall short of genuine "self"-distillation. In this study, we show that on-policy self-distillation can be achieved using only a model's own generations via internal consistency. We propose Unsupervised On-Policy Self-Distillation (U-OPSD). U-OPSD first samples multiple rollouts and constructs a pseudo-solution by majority vote under a self-consistency threshold. It then conditions a teacher distribution on the shortest pseudo-solution and distills it into prefixes of the model's longest incorrect completion, allowing the model to correct itself precisely where it is confidently wrong. Across diverse benchmarks, base models, and training settings, U-OPSD consistently improves over the base models and matches or surpasses supervised methods with ground truth (GT), such as OPSD and GRPO. On AIME24, AIME25, HMMT25, MATH500, and AMC23, U-OPSD improves over the base model by 8.5% and 10.7% on Qwen3 non-thinking mode at the 4B and 8B scales, respectively, and outperforms OPSD by an average of 3.2% and 2.3%. In thinking mode, U-OPSD remains on par with OPSD, outperforming it by 0.9% at 4B and matching it at 8B, while surpassing GRPO by 0.7% and 1.1%, respectively.

</details>


### [72] [RRC: Unlocking Generative Reward Models in LLM Reinforcement Learning via Ranking-Based Reward Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06310)
*Chenglong Wang,Ziming Zhu,Yifu Huo,Bei Li,Qiaozhi He,Yan Ding,Xiaoyang Hao,Yuxin Gao,Tianhua Zhou,Xiaojia Chang,Tongran Liu,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RRC（基于排序的奖励构建）方法，解决生成式奖励模型在强化学习中的奖励信号不匹配问题，通过自竞争排序和锚点引导排序两种策略，从相对偏好排序中派生奖励，在开放式对话和推理基准上显著提升RL训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励建模领域正从判别式奖励模型向生成式奖励模型转变，但生成式奖励模型虽然在响应排序方面表现出色，却未能在强化学习（RL）中发挥其潜力。分析发现，根本原因在于生成式奖励建模的比较性本质与现有RL算法采用的标量打分范式之间存在不匹配。

Method: 提出Ranking-based Reward Construction (RRC)方法，包含两种互补策略：(1) 自竞争排序（self-competitive ranking）：利用采样响应之间的两两比较来构建奖励；(2) 锚点引导排序（anchor-guided ranking）：利用少量参考响应实现可扩展的基于排序的奖励构建。

Result: 在开放式聊天和推理基准上的实验表明，RRC显著改善了生成式奖励模型下的RL训练，相较现有奖励构建方法取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过从相对偏好排序中派生奖励信号，RRC有效弥合了生成式奖励模型与RL算法之间的范式差距，释放了生成式奖励模型在强化学习中的潜力。

Abstract: Recent advances in reward modeling show a paradigm shift from discriminative reward models to generative reward models. However, despite their strong capabilities in response ranking, generative reward models have not realized their potential in reinforcement learning (RL). Our analysis reveals that this limitation arises from a mismatch between the comparative nature of generative reward modeling and the scalar scoring paradigm adopted by existing RL algorithms. To bridge this gap, we propose a Ranking-based Reward Construction (RRC) approach, which enables generative reward models to provide more effective RL learning signals by deriving rewards from relative preference rankings. RRC introduces two complementary strategies: self-competitive ranking, which exploits comparisons among sampled responses, and anchor-guided ranking, which enables scalable ranking-based reward construction with a small set of reference responses. Experiments across open-ended chat and reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that RRC substantially improves RL training with generative reward models, achieving consistent gains over existing reward construction approaches. Our code can be found at https://github.com/wangclnlp/RRC.

</details>


### [73] [CalibForge: Adversarial Solver Calibration for Scaling Learnable Terminal Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06352)
*Fanzhe Meng,Guoxin Chen,Jiale Zhao,Shuang Sun,Zhiyu Lin,Wayne Xin Zhao,Ruihua Song,Ji-Rong Wen,Kai Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CalibForge，一个自主终端任务合成系统，利用求解器行为通过对抗性校准来修正候选任务，构建了5,431个校准终端任务，训练出的模型在Terminal-Bench 2.0等基准上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练终端智能体需要可执行且可验证的任务，但仅凭可执行性验证无法揭示任务相对于特定求解器设置的难度是否合适。作者旨在解决如何构造既可行又具有适当挑战性的训练任务这一问题。

Method: CalibForge系统包含两种校准策略：(1)多求解器校准，针对异构求解器池中的分歧；(2)对比求解器校准，针对指定的强通过/弱失败关系。两者都以已证明的可解性为基础，操作化求解器相对的可学习区域，通过验证的求解器行为来修正候选任务。

Result: 构建了5,431个校准终端任务。在Terminal-Bench 2.0上达到32.58%和47.57%的准确率。相比对应基础模型，在Terminal-Bench 2.0上最大提升24.71个百分点，在SWE-bench Pro上提升27.68个百分点，在Doc2Repo上提升30.04个百分点。消融实验显示两种校准策略均优于仅靠创作和验证或普通单求解器反馈。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，求解器相对的可学习性可作为构建有效且可迁移的智能体训练数据的实用目标，支持了基于求解器行为的对抗性校准方法在终端任务合成中的有效性。

Abstract: Training terminal agents requires executable and verifiable tasks that are not merely solvable, but appropriately challenging for learning. Executable validation establishes feasibility, yet does not reveal how a task behaves relative to a given solver setting. In this paper, we present CalibForge, an autonomous terminal-task synthesis system that uses verified solver behavior to revise candidate tasks through adversarial solver calibration. Multi-solver calibration targets disagreement within a heterogeneous solver pool, whereas contrastive solver calibration targets a designated strong-pass/weak-fail relation; both operationalize a solver-relative learnable zone anchored in demonstrated solvability. Using CalibForge, we construct 5,431 calibrated terminal tasks. Our ablations show that both strategies yield more effective supervision than authoring and validation alone or ordinary single-solver feedback. Models trained on the full collection achieve 32.58% and 47.57% on Terminal-Bench 2.0. The largest improvements over the corresponding base model reach 24.71 percentage points on Terminal-Bench 2.0, 27.68 points on SWE-bench Pro, and 30.04 points on Doc2Repo. Together, these results support solver-relative learnability as a practical target for constructing effective and transferable agent training data.

</details>


### [74] [An Optimal Agnostic PAC Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06363)
*Markus Engelund Mathiasen,Jian Qian,Nikita Zhivotovskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对有限VC维的假设类，构造了一个经验风险最小化学习者，首次实现了与现有下界（常数因子内）匹配的统计最优风险界，解决了agnostic PAC学习在固定L*下的样本复杂度问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，agnostic PAC学习中经验风险最小化的最优风险界是否能够达到Devroye等人提出的下界（包括L*√(d/n)和d/n两项）一直是一个未解决的问题。上界与下界之间存在常数或对数因子的差距，本文旨在填补这一空白，证明该界在常数因子内不可再改进。

Method: 构造了一个基于经验风险最小化（ERM）的学习者，并利用VC维相关的不等式与精细的浓度分析，推导出包含L*√(d+log(1/δ))/n与(d+log(1/δ))/n两项的紧致风险上界。关键在于对ERM误差的精细刻画，使得上界与已知的统计下界在常数因子内精确匹配。

Result: 证明了对任意有限VC维d的假设类，所构造的学习者在n个独立同分布样本下，以至少1-δ的概率达到L(ĥ) ≤ L* + 7·10^8(√(L*(d+log(1/δ))/n) + (d+log(1/δ))/n)的风险界，与Devroye等人给出的下界在通用常数因子内匹配，从而解决了样本复杂度的紧性问题。

Conclusion: 该结果彻底解决了agnostic PAC学习中经验风险最小化的统计最优性，证明在固定L*意义下，ERM类学习算法在常数因子内已达到最优样本复杂度，为模式识别理论提供了闭合的紧下界/上界匹配。

Abstract: Let $H\subseteq\{-1,+1\}^X$ be a class of finite VC dimension $d\ge1$. Writing $L$ for the binary risk and $L^*=\min_{h\in H}L(h)$, we construct a learner achieving the statistically optimal risk bound: from an i.i.d.\ sample of size $n$, for every $0<δ\le 1/2$, with probability at least $1-δ$, \[
  L(\widehat h)
  \le L^*+ 7\cdot10^8\left(
  \sqrt{\frac{L^*(d+\log(1/δ))}{n}}
  +\frac{d+\log(1/δ)}{n}
  \right). \] This settles the sample complexity of agnostic PAC learning up to universal constants at every fixed $L^*$, matching the lower bounds of Devroye, Györfi, and Lugosi [A Probabilistic Theory of Pattern Recognition, Springer, 1996].

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [75] [Hierarchical Server Architecture for Agentic Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05332)
*Vanessa Sochat,Daniel Milroy*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向Agentic科学的分层动态资源发现架构，通过秘书代理实现对跨云、边缘和HPC系统的并发异步协商、选择与调度，在51个真实及模拟提供商（7类）上完成19,973次协商和6,952次选择模拟，达到87.71%的协商精度，且选择成本与传统策略相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic科学正改变计算工作负载的形态，这些工作负载需要跨机构、跨类型的专用硬件资源。若在调度前需要评估工作负载需求与环境匹配度，则资源的自动化发现是不可或缺的前提步骤。

Method: 设计了一种分层、动态的架构与软件系统，使用秘书代理（secretary agents）实现并发、异步的协商、选择和请求分发。代理对51个真实及模拟提供商（覆盖7个类别）进行探测与发现，并通过大量模拟（19,973次协商、6,952次选择）评估决策可靠性。

Result: 在19,973次协商模拟中达到87.71%的高协商精度；选择成本与传统调度策略相当；架构已实际支持Genesis Mission项目的运行。

Conclusion: 该架构通过代理、发现工具与基础设施之间的精心协调，证明了其在Agentic科学中的重要性，同时具备良好的可扩展性，已在真实任务中部署验证。

Abstract: Agentic science is transforming the landscape of computational work, extending to scientific pipelines and workload managers. The workloads require specialized hardware within and across institutions. If assessing workload needs against environments is required for scheduling, automated discovery of resources is an essential step. In this paper, we present a hierarchical, dynamic architecture and software to discover resources across diverse cloud, edge, and HPC systems. The design enables concurrent, asynchronous negotiation, selection, and dispatch of requests for work using secretary agents. The agents probe and discover 51 real and simulated providers across 7 categories. We perform 19,973 negotiation and 6,952 selection simulations to assess reliability of decisions, demonstrating high (87.71\%) negotiation accuracy and selection costs comparable to more traditional strategies. Designed for extensibility and currently supporting the Genesis Mission, this architecture exemplifies the importance of careful coordination between agents, discovery tools, and infrastructure for agentic science.

</details>


### [76] [Application Failures and Machine Computational Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05408)
*Carlo Graziani,Bethany Lusch,O. E. Bronson Messer*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个评估Exascale级科学计算平台在应用故障率显著时正常运行效率的框架，将故障率和平均故障间隔时间从基于时间改为基于使用量（如节点小时），并扩展了Daly（2006）的检查点框架以优化检查点使用间隔，最终利用ORNL Frontier超级计算机一年的生产运行数据进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有领导级科学计算平台和大型AI训练设施面临可观的应用程序故障率，且科学计算平台应用具有高度异构性，传统的基于时间的故障率和平均故障间隔时间（MTBI）指标无法准确反映这种多样性带来的实际故障影响，需要一种基于使用量的新评估框架。

Method: 将故障率和平均故障间隔时间从基于时间改为基于使用量（如节点小时）来定义；考虑故障、检查点和重启造成的使用量损失，更新Daly（2006）框架，允许用户指定最优检查点使用间隔以最小化损失；推导机器计算效率以量化可用于科学计算的期望资源分配比例；使用Frontier超级计算机一年的生产运行数据进行实证验证。

Result: 成功建立了基于使用量的Exascale系统正常运行效率评估框架，并通过Frontier超级计算机的真实生产数据展示了方法论的实际应用效果。

Conclusion: 对于科学计算平台，故障率和MTBI应以使用量而非时间定义，基于该框架可以更准确地评估此类系统的计算效率和优化检查点策略。

Abstract: We present a framework for evaluating uptime efficiency of Exascale-class scientific computers when application failure rates are appreciable. This is the situation that confronts current leadership-class scientific computing platforms and large AI training installations. What distinguishes scientific computing platforms is the heterogeneity of their applications. We argue that this diversity requires that failure rates and mean intervals between failures should be specified in terms of \emph{usage} (e.g. node-hours) rather than time, as is currently customary. We consider the usage loss terms due to failures, to checkpointing, and to restart costs, and update the framework of Daly (2006) allowing users to specify optimal checkpointing usage intervals that minimize such losses. We derive the machine computational efficiency, which specifies the expected fractional resource allocation that is available for scientific computation. We illustrate the methodology using one year of production runtime data from the \emph{Frontier} supercomputer at Oak Ridge National Laboratory.

</details>


### [77] [Serverless platform driven CPU loadbalancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05633)
*Abdul Rehman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个由Serverless平台驱动的CPU负载均衡框架，通过基于SchedExt(SCX)构建自定义Linux调度器，使控制平面能够直接干预CPU调度。该调度器引入可配置调度域和共享接口，根据函数历史到达间隔将函数分配到不同域，域内采用单队列负载均衡与虚拟时间优先级策略。实验表明八域配置可将能耗降低约15%，调用成本仅增加5%，重负载下请求延迟比默认CFS调度器最多降低50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Serverless平台掌握全局函数调用和资源利用信息，但现有的CPU调度决策仍被限制在操作系统调度器层面，控制平面的全局视角无法被充分利用来优化调度效率。

Method: 1) 基于SchedExt(SCX)构建自定义Linux调度器，使Serverless控制平面能直接影响CPU调度；2) 引入可配置的调度域（scheduling domains）和共享接口，控制平面依据函数历史到达间隔将函数分配到合适的域；3) 每个域内采用单队列负载均衡策略与虚拟时间优先级化策略，优化任务放置并减少忙轮询（busy-polling）任务的干扰。

Result: 1) 八个域的配置取得最佳权衡：系统能耗降低约15%，调用成本仅增加5%；2) 在重负载场景下，请求延迟相比默认Linux CFS调度器最多降低50%。

Conclusion: 将CPU调度决策暴露给Serverless控制平面，能够在保持调度灵活性的同时提升能量效率和工作负载性能，展示了平台级调度相较于纯操作系统级调度的优势。

Abstract: Serverless platforms maintain a global view of function invocations and resource utilization, yet existing systems largely restrict CPU scheduling decisions to the operating system scheduler. This paper presents a serverless platform-driven CPU load balancing framework that enables the control plane to directly influence CPU scheduling through a custom Linux scheduler built on SchedExt(SCX). The proposed scheduler introduces configurable scheduling domains and a shared interface that allows the control plane to assign functions to domains based on their historical inter-arrival times. Within each domain, a single-queue load-balancing strategy combined with a virtual-time prioritization policy improves task placement while reducing interference from busy-polling tasks. Results show that an eight-domain configuration achieves the best trade-off, reducing system energy consumption by approximately 15% while increasing invocation cost by only 5%. Under heavily loaded workloads, the proposed scheduler also reduces request latency by up to 50% compared to the default Linux Completely Fair Scheduler (CFS). These results demonstrate that exposing CPU scheduling decisions to the serverless control plane can improve both energy efficiency and workload performance while preserving scheduling flexibility.

</details>


### [78] [RepoOMP: Repository-Aware Hotspot OpenMP Parallelization via Dependency-Aware Context Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05855)
*Yongjie Qian,Ke Gao,Zhibin Zhang,Shaohui Peng,Ling Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RepoOMP是一个面向成熟代码库中热点并行化的混合框架，通过构建多粒度属性性能图(MAP)恢复并行化相关证据，在确定性规则和LLM智能体之间路由热点，并构造结构化变换上下文(STC)向模型暴露依赖事实而不淹没无关仓库文本。在951个来自NPB、BOTS、FFmpeg、NCNN和GROMACS的热点上评估，372个被接受（含330个真实仓库热点），NPB平均加速8.23倍，BOTS平均加速8.96倍，真实内核跨骨干平均加速5.25倍，相比非结构化Claude Code基线提速18-28%并降低47-68%的token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成熟代码库中热点并行化的OpenMP转换仍很困难，因为循环安全性和优化收益常依赖于非局部证据。规则工具在合法性无法局部证明时并行化不足，而基于智能体的方法在检索遗漏关键依赖或包含无关代码时变得不稳定。需要一个能先恢复并行化相关证据再生成代码的混合框架。

Method: RepoOMP采用混合框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 构建多粒度属性性能图(Multi-granularity Attributes Performance graph, MAP)以恢复并行化相关证据；2) 在确定性规则和LLM智能体之间路由热点，确保局部可证明的情况由规则处理而复杂情况交由智能体；3) 构造结构化变换上下文(Structured Transformation Context, STC)，向模型暴露依赖事实而不将无关仓库文本注入模型。

Result: 在951个来自NPB、BOTS、FFmpeg、NCNN和GROMACS的profiled热点上评估。在编译、工作负载特定检查和正向加速条件下，372个热点被接受，包括330个真实仓库热点。NPB平均加速8.23倍，BOTS平均加速8.96倍。九个详细真实世界内核上跨骨干平均加速5.25倍，相对Claude Code基线提速18-28%，减少47-68%的agent端token成本。330个被接受的真实仓库热点中位加速2.25倍。

Conclusion: RepoOMP提供了一个证据引导的仓库场景下热点并行化工作流，通过恢复非局部证据、混合路由决策和结构化上下文构造，有效解决了规则工具过度保守和智能体方法不稳定的问题，并显著提升并行化加速比与token效率。代码已开源。

Abstract: OpenMP parallelization of hotspots in mature repositories remains difficult because loop safety and optimization payoff often depend on non-local evidence. Rule-based tools under-parallelize when legality is not locally provable, while agent-based approaches become unstable when retrieval misses decisive dependencies or includes irrelevant code. We present RepoOMP, a hybrid framework that recovers parallelization-relevant evidence before generation. RepoOMP builds a Multi-granularity Attributes Performance graph (MAP), routes hotspots between deterministic rules and an LLM agent, and constructs a Structured Transformation Context (STC) that exposes dependency facts without flooding the model with unrelated repository text. We evaluate RepoOMP on 951 profiled hotspots from NPB, BOTS, FFmpeg, NCNN, and GROMACS. Under compilation, workload-specific checks, and positive speedup, 372 hotspots are accepted, including 330 real-world repository hotspots. RepoOMP achieves average speedups of $8.23\times$ on NPB and $8.96\times$ on BOTS. For the nine detailed real-world kernels used in matched-backbone and robustness analyses, RepoOMP reaches a cross-backbone mean of $5.25\times$, improves speedup by 18--28\%, and reduces agent-side token cost by 47--68\% relative to the unstructured Claude Code baseline. Across 330 accepted real-world hotspots, median speedup is $2.25\times$. Overall, RepoOMP provides an evidence-guided workflow for hotspot parallelization in repository settings. The open-source repository is available at https://github.com/Qlalq/RepoOMP_Simplified.

</details>


### [79] [Operating Multi-Node Full Fine-Tuning on NVIDIA B300: A Field Report on Telemetry-Based Triage, Negative Results, and Operational Hardening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05944)
*Seon Ho Kim,Ui Jeong Jeon,Su Hyeon Kim,Min Tae Hwang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文报告了在16块NVIDIA B300（两节点FSDP/ZeRO-3）上全量微调32.76B稠密模型（Qwen3-32B）的实战运营经验。作者宣称不提出新算法，而是提供一套在新硬件上校准的集成现场经验和测量数据，包含四个实践者工件：B300校准的功耗诊断表、一组诚实的负面结果（破除常见优化迷思）、4/8/16 GPU强扩展基准数据，以及一个epoch末NCCL死锁的故障案例及解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为大规模模型微调在新一代NVIDIA B300硬件上的实际操作提供首份公开的现场经验记录和校准测量参考数据。由于B300是新硬件，缺乏可参考的实践经验，作者旨在通过系统性记录实践过程和失败案例，帮助从业者避免常见陷阱，并澄清关于性能优化的常见误解。

Method: 在16×NVIDIA B300（两节点FSDP/ZeRO-3）上全量微调Qwen3-32B（32.76B参数稠密模型）。具体方法包括：(1) 通过板级功耗读数构建诊断分类表，区分计算/通信/数据饥饿/检查点死锁/空闲等状态；(2) 设计对照实验（A/B测试）验证NFS读取与本地预分词缓存的吞吐差异，并重构早前"吞吐崩塌"事件根因；(3) 系统测量4/8/16 GPU强扩展的绝对数值与GPU小时数；(4) 针对per-rank token打包不平衡导致的epoch末NCCL死锁，设计了2.7秒的预运行不变量门控检查和外部watcher监控机制，对应PyTorch的Join/equalize-to-minimum实践。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 功耗读数比利用率百分比更能诊断问题——在NCCL挂起时利用率显示100%而功耗数据能揭示真实状态；(2) 在该规模下per-step NFS读取与本地预分词缓存性能相当（约53k tok/s），因为语料适合页缓存且任务为计算密集，推翻了存储介质是瓶颈的常见假设；"吞吐崩塌"实为NFS/CPU竞争而非存储限制；(3) B300上4/8/16 GPU强扩展接近线性，符合该规模预期；(4) 死锁故障由per-rank token打包不平衡引发，2.7秒预运行门控和外部watcher可将近小时级的静默故障转化为即时拒绝，大幅节省GPU小时。

Conclusion: 可迁移的核心经验是运营层面的而非算法层面的：对于数据依赖的数据并行任务，应关注功耗而非利用率指标来判断系统状态，并在启动前验证不变量——通过冒烟测试并不能证明完整运行是安全的。

Abstract: We report operational experience full-fine-tuning a 32.76B-parameter dense model (Qwen3-32B) on 16 x NVIDIA B300 (two nodes, FSDP / ZeRO-3) -- among the first published field accounts on this accelerator. We claim no new algorithm. The individual mechanisms we use are established practice; our contribution is the integrated field experience and a set of calibrated measurements on new hardware. Concretely we offer four practitioner artifacts. (1) A B300-calibrated power-draw triage table that distinguishes compute / communication / data-starvation / checkpoint-or-deadlock / idle by board wattage (utilization% reads 100% during an NCCL hang). (2) A set of honest negative results that dispel common optimization folklore at this scale: a controlled A/B in which per-step NFS reading matches a pretokenized local cache (~53k tok/s) because the corpus fits in page cache and the job is compute-bound; and a reconstruction of an earlier "throughput collapse" as NFS/CPU contention rather than a storage-medium limit. (3) Calibrated 4/8/16-GPU strong-scaling and GPU-hour numbers on B300 (near-linear, as expected in this regime; we report absolute values as reference data). (4) A worked failure case -- an epoch-end NCCL deadlock from per-rank token-packing imbalance -- together with a 2.7-second pre-run invariant gate and an external watcher that turn multi-hour silent failures into instant rejections. This deadlock and its remedy correspond to PyTorch's documented Join / equalize-to-minimum practice; we position our instantiation against that prior art and report the GPU-hours the failure cost and the gate saves. The transferable takeaway is operational, not algorithmic: for data-dependent data-parallel jobs, watch power rather than utilization, and verify invariants before launch -- a passing smoke test is not evidence of a safe full run.

</details>


### [80] [TensorCast: The Missing Tensor Management Layer in Large Language Model Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06007)
*Yuhan Zhou,Yuchu Luo,Hao Nie,Wangrunze Lv,Yu Zhou,Yibo Zhu,Daxin Jiang,Chenren Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Tensor-as-a-Service (TaaS)及其实现TensorCast，将张量生命周期管理从计算逻辑中解耦出来，作为LLM基础设施中的新抽象层。TensorCast提供了头等张量抽象、可编程生命周期原语和分离策略与机制的运行时，支持分布式张量管理程序的编写。在与vLLM和SGLang集成后，在多类张量生命周期工作负载上达到与专用系统相当的性能，并在多轮Agent并发场景下通过可编程策略将TTFT中位数提升最多93.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基础设施中的张量不仅是计算数据，还作为跨分布式组件共享的持久状态。现有系统针对模型权重加载、KV缓存管理、检查点同步等任务进行深度定制优化，但这种特化产生了隔离的"孤岛"，阻碍了张量管理策略在不同LLM工作负载间的复用与组合。作者认为张量生命周期管理是LLM基础设施中缺失的抽象层。

Method: 提出Tensor-as-a-Service (TaaS)抽象，将张量状态管理与计算逻辑解耦。实现TensorCast分布式张量管理层，包含三个核心设计：(1) 头等张量抽象（first-class tensor abstractions）；(2) 可编程生命周期原语（programmable lifecycle primitives）；(3) 将张量管理策略与执行机制分离的运行时。开发者通过TensorCast API编写张量管理程序，透明地利用分布式执行与数据移动。系统集成到vLLM和SGLang中。

Result: TensorCast在模型权重物化、权重同步、KV缓存管理和可编程请求路由等多样张量生命周期工作负载上，性能与专用张量管理系统相当；同时支持新的跨组件优化策略。在高度并发的多轮Agent工作负载下，使用TensorCast实现的可编程策略将中位数TTFT提升最多93.2%。

Conclusion: 张量生命周期管理应作为LLM基础设施中的独立抽象层，而非被各专门系统割裂处理。TaaS/TensorCast证明了将张量管理策略与执行机制解耦，既能保持与专用系统竞争的性能，又能实现跨组件优化的新可能性，为应对不断演进的LLM工作负载提供了更灵活、可组合的基础设施方向。

Abstract: Modern LLM infrastructure increasingly manages tensors not only as computation data, but also as persistent states shared across distributed components. Existing systems optimize individual tensor management tasks, such as model weight loading, KV cache management, and checkpoint synchronization, by deeply integrating task-specific mechanisms with execution engines, networks, or storage backends. However, this specialization creates isolated silos that hinder the reuse and composition of tensor management strategies across evolving LLM workloads. In this paper, we identify tensor lifecycle management as a missing abstraction layer in LLM infrastructure and propose Tensor-as-a-Service (TaaS), which decouples tensor state management from computation logic. We design and build TensorCast, a distributed tensor management layer that provides first-class tensor abstractions, programmable lifecycle primitives, and a runtime that separates tensor management policies from execution mechanisms. This enables developers to write tensor management programs using TensorCast APIs while transparently leveraging distributed execution and data movement. We integrate TensorCast with vLLM and SGLang and evaluate it across diverse tensor lifecycle workloads, including model weight materialization, weight synchronization, KV cache management, and programmable request routing. Our results show that TensorCast achieves competitive performance with specialized tensor management systems while enabling new cross-component optimization policies. A programmable policy implemented with TensorCast improves median TTFT by up to 93.2% under highly concurrent multi-turn agent workloads.

</details>


### [81] [Routing LLM Inference to the Cleanest Grid in Real Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06188)
*Aleks Bernhard,Arif Baran Yardimci*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对大规模语言模型推理中的碳排放感知路由进行实时验证，通过多区域GPU测试平台上的MOER信号，在无需重新训练或硬件变更的情况下实现碳优化调度，并在年度模拟中实现50.9%的GPU可归因运营排放削减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理是快速增长的电力负荷，其边际碳强度在不同电网区域和一天内不同时间差异超过一个数量级。请求放置（request placement）成为有吸引力的减碳杠杆，因为它无需重新训练或硬件更换。然而，已有工作缺乏实际生产环境验证，未使用真实能源遥测数据，也未对每个请求基于历史MOER进行碳结算。

Method: 在多区域GPU测试平台上进行实时验证，使用边际运行排放率（MOER）信号驱动推理路由。关键方法包括：(1) 以实际生产的基于压力的路由器作为盲基线（而非均匀放置）；(2) 通过NVIDIA DCGM遥测和实测并发曲线按请求归因计算能耗（而非标称TDP）；(3) 每个请求依据历史MOER进行碳结算，而非仅使用驱动决策的预测值。此外，对横跨美国电网多样性的车队进行一年的小时级MOER回放模拟。

Result: 实时验证表明MOER信号可跨区域引导推理且无调度失败，作为生产路由器的严格且可逆的叠加层。在主要模拟配置中，与轮询调度相比，碳感知放置将GPU可归因运营排放减少50.9%（95%区块自助置信区间48.5-53.3%）。由于回放使用历史MOER而非预测，此结果为该配置下的上界。会话固定前，小时级最低MOER路由在年度均值MOER静态策略基础上额外贡献约22.4个百分点（占54.0%放置减排的约40%）。

Conclusion: 碳感知推理路由在真实生产环境中是可行的，可作为现有路由器的严格叠加层。减排效果显著但应视为特定车队和历史年份的模型结果而非普遍估计。实用建议：区域间比较应依据绝对MOER而非区域内归一化的百分位信号指数，因为后者回答的是时间性而非空间性问题。

Abstract: Large-language-model inference is a fast-growing electricity load whose marginal carbon intensity varies by more than an order of magnitude across grid regions and across the day, making request placement an attractive lever: no retraining, no hardware change. We report a live validation of carbon-aware inference routing on multi-region GPU testbeds driven by marginal operating emissions rate (MOER) signals, with three properties uncommon in prior work: a blind baseline that is an actual production pressure-based router rather than uniform placement; per-request energy attributed from GPU telemetry (NVIDIA DCGM) via measured concurrency curves rather than nameplate TDP; and carbon settlement of every request against historical MOER, not only the forecast that drove the decision. The central live result is feasibility: a MOER signal steered inference across regions with no observed dispatch failures, as a strict and reversible overlay on the production router. To size the effect, we replay a year of hourly MOER across a grid-diverse CONUS fleet. In the primary modeled configuration, carbon-aware placement reduces modeled GPU-attributable operational emissions by 50.9% versus round-robin (95% block-bootstrap CI 48.5-53.3%). Because the replay dispatches against historical MOER rather than a forecast, this is an upper bound under that configuration; forecast error would reduce operationally realized savings. Before session pinning, hourly lowest-MOER routing contributes about 22.4 percentage points, roughly 40% of the 54.0% placement reduction, beyond a static annual-mean-MOER policy. These are modeled results for one fleet and historical year, not a universal estimate. We also record a practical observation: when comparing regions, rank by absolute MOER rather than the percentile signal-index, which is normalized within each region and answers a temporal, not a spatial, question.

</details>

### [82] [Agentic Nesting: A New Methodology for Existing Enterprise Application Integration and Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05159)
*Xi Wang,Kun Li,Xianyao Ling,Gang Yin,Liang Zhang,Jiang Wu,Wenbo Lei,Jun Xu,Annie Wang,Fu Zhang,Weizhe Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Nesting，一种多智能体协作框架，将现有企业应用封装为自主AI智能体并组织成分层嵌套结构，通过"应用即智能体"的集成范式和"对话即集成"的交互理念，实现跨应用的统一对话式编排与企业系统协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业运营依赖多个异构业务系统和信息应用，导致严重的数据孤岛与流程碎片化。传统集成方法（ESB中间件、API网关、RPA）存在架构耦合度高、运维成本攀升、智能化能力有限等固有缺陷，难以有效编排和利用已投入大量资源建设的企业应用。

Method: 提出Agentic Nesting多智能体协作框架：1）将遗留企业应用封装为具有层级嵌套结构的自主AI代理（Application-as-Agent）；2）从每个遗留系统中提取数字代理代理层，支持自然语言交互和自主操作；3）通过中央编排器协调多个智能体，实现任务分解与动态调度；4）暴露统一的对话式接口，支持跨应用查询和流程编排（Conversation-as-Integration）。智能体按分层管理拓扑组织，而非扁平互联，以匹配企业生态的组合复杂性。

Result: 论文的主要贡献在于提出了"应用即智能体"（Application-as-Agent）的集成范式和"对话即集成"（Conversation-as-Integration）的交互理念，并探索了该方法在异构系统协调和大规模数据应用场景中的泛化潜力。

Conclusion: Agentic Nesting通过层级嵌套的多智能体架构，为传统企业应用集成提供了智能化替代方案，有望解决数据孤岛和流程碎片化问题，其泛化能力值得在更广泛的企业场景中进一步验证。

Abstract: Enterprise operations extensively rely on multiple heterogeneous business systems and information applications, which also result in severe data silos and process fragmentation. Enterprises have invested considerable financial and material resources in building these applications, however, effectively leveraging and orchestrating them remains a formidable challenge. Conventional approaches to enterprise application integration, encompassing middleware architectures such as Enterprise Service Bus (ESB), API gateway infrastructures, and Robotic Process Automation (RPA), suffer from inherent limitations like high architectural coupling, escalating operation and maintenance costs, and limited intelligence capabilities. This paper proposes Agentic Nesting, a multi-agent collaboration framework in which existing enterprise applications are encapsulated as autonomous AI agents within a hierarchically nested structure. Rather than flat interconnection, agents are organized into layered stewardship topologies that mirror the compositional complexity of enterprise ecosystems. The framework extracts a digital agent proxy from each legacy application to enable natural-language interaction and autonomous manipulation, coordinates multiple agents through a central orchestrator for task decomposition and dynamic dispatching, and exposes a unified conversational interface for cross-application querying and process orchestration. The main contributions of this paper are the proposition of the "Application-as-Agent" integration paradigm and the "Conversation-as-Integration" interaction philosophy, together with an exploration of the generalization potential of this methodology in scenarios encompassing heterogeneous system coordination, and large-scale data applications.

</details>


### [83] [The Ignition Index: Measuring Global Workspace Dynamics in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05160)
*Saman Rahbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Ignition Index (I)指标，通过四参数sigmoid拟合每层线性探针准确率随输入信号强度的变化，提取陡峭度参数beta-hat来量化transformer语言模型中全局工作空间理论(GWT)的全有或全无点火预测。在11个模型、5个架构家族上验证，具有9.6倍测量选择性，并发现前馈transformer比SSM高89%的点火强度、循环架构沿迭代轴显现工作空间样转变、以及训练step 256处的相变等关键结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局工作空间理论(GWT)预测神经网络应表现出全有或全无的点火现象，但此前缺乏定量指标在语言模型中操作化并验证这一预测。现有研究未能将GWT的动态预测与机制可解释性之间建立实证桥梁。

Method: 提出Ignition Index指标：对每层线性探针准确率与输入信号强度的关系拟合四参数sigmoid，提取陡峭度参数beta-hat作为点火强度度量。通过打乱标签对照实验评估选择性，在11个模型（含5个架构家族：前馈transformer、SSM/Mamba、循环架构Huginn、Pythia等）上系统测量，并使用PELT检测训练过程中的相变。

Result: (1) 打乱标签对照显示9.6倍选择性(p<0.001)；(2) 前馈transformer比SSM聚合beta-hat高89%(p<1e-13, d=0.52)，Mamba呈近线性特征表明缺乏全局广播；(3) Huginn-3.5B沿迭代轴比深度轴高2.12倍点火，循环架构在递归维度表现工作空间样转变；(4) Pythia-410M在训练step 256检测到相变(+67%)，先于归纳头形成；(5) 点火与模型规模和信号强度的假设未获支持。

Conclusion: Ignition Index是首个在Transformer语言模型中定量验证GWT动态预测的指标，具有高选择性（9.6倍）和架构层面可区分性。研究揭示不同架构家族在信息整合模式上的本质差异，但点火机制可能已在前馈transformer中饱和，且与规模无关。

Abstract: We introduce the Ignition Index (I), a validated scalar metric that operationalizes Global Workspace Theory's (GWT) all-or-none ignition prediction in transformer language models. The metric fits a four-parameter sigmoid to per-layer linear probe accuracy as a function of input signal strength, extracting steepness parameter beta-hat: high values indicate abrupt, ignition-like transitions; low values indicate graded build-up. Across 11 models spanning five architecture families, shuffled-label controls demonstrate 9.6-fold selectivity for genuine linguistic structure over spurious probe capacity (p < 0.001, Mann-Whitney U-test). We find: (1) Feedforward transformers exceed SSMs by 89% in aggregate beta-hat (p < 1e-13, Cohen's d = 0.52), with Mamba exhibiting near-linear profiles consistent with absent global broadcast. (2) Huginn-3.5B exhibits 2.12-fold higher ignition along its iteration axis than its depth axis, demonstrating that recurrent architectures manifest workspace-like transitions along the recurrence dimension. (3) Pythia-410M shows a PELT-detected phase transition at training step 256 (+67%), preceding induction-head formation. (4) Hypotheses linking ignition to model scale and signal strength were not confirmed, suggesting transformer architectures may saturate available ignition mechanisms. The Ignition Index provides the first validated quantitative bridge between GWT's dynamical predictions and mechanistic interpretability, with 9.6-fold measurement selectivity and architecture-level discriminability not previously characterized in the scaling literature. Code: https://github.com/saman-rahbar/ignition-index

</details>


### [84] [Woodpecker Distillation: Weak Models Diagnose Reasoning Bugs in Strong Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05168)
*Dayu Wang,Jiaye Yang,Weikang Li,Jiahui Liang,Yang Li,Deguo Xia,Jizhou Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Woodpecker Distillation框架，通过对比性局部干预（成功与不成功补丁）构建纠正性教师分布，将弱模型干预的信号蒸馏到强模型中，从而修复大语言模型在推理任务中的局部推理错误，超越直接模仿基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在推理任务中经常失败，但这些失败往往源于中间步骤的局部推理缺陷，而非全局能力不足。这些缺陷可通过插入弱模型生成的短补丁来修复，但直接微调弱补丁或修复轨迹无法可靠地内化这种纠正效果，表明有效信号不在于干预文本本身，而在于干预如何重塑模型未来的推理分布。

Method: 提出Woodpecker Distillation框架：(1) 在同一前缀上对比成功和不成功的弱模型补丁；(2) 利用这些对比补丁诱导的未来token预测构建纠正性教师分布；(3) 将这一信号蒸馏到强模型中。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的实验表明，Woodpecker Distillation持续提升强模型性能，并且优于直接模仿的基线方法。

Conclusion: 局部干预信号的价值不在于干预文本本身，而在于其重塑未来推理分布的方式；通过对比性局部干预进行蒸馏可以有效提升强模型的推理能力。

Abstract: Large language models often fail on reasoning tasks despite possessing the capability to solve them. We argue that many such failures arise from localized reasoning bugs in intermediate steps rather than from global incompetence. We show that these bugs are frequently repairable: inserting a short patch generated by a weak probe model after the same strong-model reasoning prefix can redirect the trajectory toward a correct solution.
  However, this corrective effect is not reliably internalized by directly fine-tuning on weak patches or repaired trajectories, suggesting that the useful signal lies not in the intervention text itself, but in how it reshapes the model's future reasoning distribution. We therefore propose Woodpecker Distillation, a weak-to-strong training framework that learns from contrastive local interventions. Our method contrasts successful and unsuccessful weak-model patches at the same prefix, constructs a corrective teacher distribution from their induced future token predictions, and distills this signal into the strong model.
  Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that Woodpecker Distillation consistently improves strong-model performance and outperforms direct imitation baselines.

</details>


### [85] [From Continuous Predictors to Clinical Thresholds: Early Evidence on Performance Trade-offs of Guideline-Based Categorisation for Ischaemic Stroke Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05203)
*Esra Zihni,Katryna Cisek,Hamzah Ziadeh,Hendrik Knoche,Robert Mikulik,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了急性缺血性卒中90天结局预测模型中，能否用临床指南对齐的分类编码替代连续预测变量而不损失性能。结果表明在两个治疗队列中完全分类模型与连续模型在统计上不可区分，全局特征重要性排序保持一致，指南驱动的分类化是可行的模型设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在急性缺血性卒中90天结局预测中虽有较强预测准确性，但临床采纳受限，原因在于模型解释与临床医生推理方式不一致。临床医生用户研究呼吁采用符合临床指南的截断值（cut-offs），因此研究者想知道是否可以在不牺牲性能的前提下，用临床指导的分类编码替代连续预测变量。

Method: 基于多中心欧洲登记数据，将患者分为三个治疗队列，比较标准梯度提升模型与完全分类化的梯度提升模型。分类化模型采用卒中指南对齐的、按治疗特异的阈值对连续预测变量进行离散化，并评估两类模型的预测性能与全局特征重要性排序。

Result: 在三个治疗队列中，完全分类化模型在其中两个队列上与连续模型在统计上无显著差异，在另一个队列上预测准确性出现显著下降。全局特征重要性排序在所有治疗组中保持一致性，表明基于指南的分类化保留了预后因素的核心层级结构。

Conclusion: 基于指南的分类化编码是卒中结局预测模型的可行设计选择，既能保持特征重要性的核心层次，又能在多数治疗队列中维持预测性能，并提升模型解释与临床推理的对齐程度。

Abstract: Machine learning models achieve strong predictive accuracy for 90-day outcome prediction in acute ischaemic stroke, yet clinical adoption is limited by the misalignment of model explanations with clinicians' reasoning. Motivated by a clinician user study calling for clinical guideline-aligned cut-offs, we ask whether continuous predictors can be replaced by clinically informed categorical encodings without sacrificing performance. On a multi-centre European registry stratified into three treatment cohorts, we compare standard and fully categorised gradient-boosted models, the latter using stroke guideline-aligned, treatment-specific thresholds. The fully categorised models are statistically indistinguishable from their continuous counterparts in two of the treatment cohorts, with a significant drop in predictive accuracy in one cohort. Global feature importance rankings remain consistent, suggesting that discretising continuous predictors into guideline-based categories preserves the core hierarchy of prognostic factors across all treatment groups. Guideline-based categorisation is thus a viable design choice for stroke-outcome models.

</details>


### [86] [SkillTrace: Multi-Trace Provenance Auditing for LLM-Agent Skill Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05204)
*Jialuo Chen,Minghe Wang,Lingqi Jiang,Jianan Ma,Xinhao Deng,Xiaohu Du,Ruixiao Lin,Yunhao Feng,Linkang Du,Jingyi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SKILLTRACE是一个针对LLM智能体技能复用的多线索溯源审计框架，通过提取表达、实现和操作三条溯源线索，在审计时进行确定性比较，在SKILLTRACE-BENCH基准上达到AUROC 0.938和F1 0.898，并在36,446个技能的大规模审计中优于仓库级基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体生态系统围绕可复用技能（混合模态的元数据、自然语言指令、代码、工具、参考和工作流包）快速增长，技能成为市场商品后，审计其复用已不同于普通代码克隆检测。现有检测器仅针对单模态源代码或整体包相似度，但技能复用证据分散在作者文本、实现片段和操作结构中，因此可能漏掉仅保留技能一部分的复用行为。

Method: SKILLTRACE提取三条溯源线索：表达线索（Expression）、实现线索（Implementation）和操作线索（Operational）。其中操作线索被表示为技能操作图（SOG），捕捉激活、流程和资源流结构。LLM仅在摄取时辅助一次操作线索的提取；审计时SKILLTRACE对缓存的线索进行确定性比较，针对同功能严格负样本校准每条线索，并报告哪条线索支持复用判定。

Result: 在SKILLTRACE-BENCH基准上（包含100个市场锚点上的820个转换复用正样本和751个负对照），SKILLTRACE达到AUROC 0.938和F1 0.898。此外，对36,446个技能的野外观测审计表明，线索归因证据能产生比仓库级基线更可操作、更值得审查的复用队列。

Conclusion: SKILLTRACE通过将复用证据分解为三条可独立判定的溯源线索，有效解决了LLM智能体技能复用的多模态、部分复用审计难题，比现有仓库级基线提供更精准、更具可操作性的复用审查结果。

Abstract: LLM-agent ecosystems are rapidly growing around reusable skills: mixed-modality packages of metadata, natural-language instructions, code, tools, references, and operational workflows. As skills become marketplace artifacts, auditing their reuse is no longer the same problem as ordinary code clone detection. Existing detectors target single-modality source code or whole-package similarity, yet skill reuse evidence is distributed across authored text, implementation fragments, and operational structure. As a result, they can miss reuse that preserves only one part of a skill. We present SKILLTRACE, a multi-trace provenance auditing framework for LLM-agent skill reuse. SKILLTRACE extracts three provenance traces: Expression, Implementation, and Operational. It represents the Operational Trace as a Skill Operational Graph (SOG) that captures activation, procedure, and resource-flow structure. An LLM assists only the Operational-trace extraction, once at ingestion; at audit time SKILLTRACE compares cached traces deterministically, calibrates each trace against same-function strict negatives, and reports which trace supports a reuse decision. On SKILLTRACE-BENCH, with 820 transformed reuse positives over 100 marketplace anchors and 751 negative controls, SKILLTRACE achieves AUROC 0.938 and F1 0.898. A 36,446-skill wild audit further shows that trace-attributed evidence surfaces actionable reuse review queues beyond repository-level baselines.

</details>


### [87] [Abstract Event Causal Rules: Induction and Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05205)
*Ziwei Zheng,Peiqiong Chen,Bang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出抽象事件因果规则（AECR）范式，将实例级因果对泛化为抽象因果逻辑以解决低频率长尾和未见事件组合的泛化缺陷，并设计多智能体具体到抽象因果归纳系统（CACI）构建知识库，通过抽象规则引导的因果注意力编码器（AR-GCAE）注入事件预测任务，显著提升事件因果推理的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件中心智能分析系统依赖显式因果事件知识，但实例级因果对在低频长尾和未见事件组合上存在严重泛化缺陷，缺乏将具体因果知识抽象为可迁移规则的能力。

Method: 提出AECR关系级因果抽象范式，将具体因果对转化为广义抽象因果逻辑；设计多智能体CACI系统结合相似度约束聚类，从噪声原始因果数据中提炼可信AECR并构建两个知识库；提出AR-GCAE编码器，通过规则引导注意力层和门控表示融合将检索到的AECR注入CGEP基准任务。

Result: 定量实验表明，应用AECR显著增强了事件因果推理的泛化能力，为事件预测带来一致的性能提升，在罕见和未见事件样本上增益最为突出。

Conclusion: 抽象因果规则知识能有效弥补实例级因果对在低频和未见事件上的泛化不足，为事件级因果推理提供了一条可行的知识抽象与注入路径。

Abstract: Event-centric intelligent analytical systems heavily depend on explicit causal event knowledge for risk early warning, decision-making support and narrative comprehension. Nevertheless, existing instance-level causal pairs suffer severe generalization deficits on low-frequency long-tail and unseen event combinations. To address this limitation, this work proposes Abstract Event Causal Rule (AECR), a novel relation-level causal abstraction paradigm that transforms concrete cause-effect pairs into generalized abstract causal logic while retaining their intrinsic causal relationships. We design a multi-agent Concrete-to-Abstract Causal Induction (CACI) system coupled with similarity-constrained clustering to distill trustworthy AECRs from noisy raw causal data, based on which two complete AECR knowledge bases are built. To validate the practical utility of abstract causal knowledge, we propose an Abstract Rule-Guided Causal Attention Encoder (AR-GCAE), which injects the retrieved AECRs into the causality Graph Event Prediction (CGEP) benchmark task via rule-guided attention layers and gated representation fusion. Quantitative experimental results reveal that applying AECRs substantially strengthens the generalization capacity of event causal reasoning and brings consistent performance improvements to event prediction, with the most prominent gains observed on rare and unseen event samples.

</details>


### [88] [Otter: A Time-Aware, History-Conditioned Human Chess AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05206)
*Tarun Kumar S*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Otter是一个15.3M参数的人类象棋AI，通过将下棋建模为时间感知的顺序过程（而非孤立处理每个局面）来预测人类走子。它在6.1B个局面（来自1.17亿场Lichess快棋对局）上训练，在top-1和top-5走子预测准确率上分别达到55.23%和90.95%，超越了此前最优的Maia 2模型，且参数量更少、训练数据更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人类走子预测模型（如Maia 2）主要将每个棋局局面视为独立位置进行处理，忽略了人类在实际下棋过程中的时间依赖性和顺序特性。论文的动机是质疑这种位置孤立的建模方式，主张将人类的象棋活动建模为时间感知的、顺序进行的过程能更准确地反映人类实际下的棋。

Method: Otter采用两个条件信号来建模人类走子：(1) 走子历史编码器——基于最近20步棋的条件预测，捕捉开局偏好、位置漂移和局内行为趋势；(2) 时间控制模块——根据时钟压力调节预测。模型在6.1B个局面（1.17亿场Lichess快棋对局）上训练，仅使用单张T4 GPU训练30天。

Result: Otter实现55.23%的top-1和90.95%的top-5走子预测准确率，在11个Elo等级（<1100到>=2000）上均超越先前SOTA模型Maia 2，其中在1900-1999等级段达到峰值57.38%的准确率，同时参数量更少、训练数据更少。

Conclusion: 将象棋建模为时间感知的顺序活动比仅基于位置的建模方法能产生更接近人类的走子预测，且可以使用更小的模型实现。代码、训练模型和完整训练日志已公开发布。

Abstract: Otter is a 15.3M-parameter human chess AI that predicts human move selection by modeling play as a time-aware, sequential process rather than treating each position in isolation. It combines two conditioning signals: (1) a move history encoder that conditions predictions on the last 20 moves, capturing opening preferences, positional drift, and intra-game behavioral tendencies; and (2) a time control module that modulates predictions based on clock pressure. Otter is trained on 6.1 billion positions from 117 million Lichess rapid games over 30 days on a single T4 GPU.
  Otter achieves 55.23% top-1 and 90.95% top-5 move-prediction accuracy, surpassing the prior state-of-the-art human chess model, Maia 2, with far fewer parameters and less training data. Across 11 Elo brackets (<1100 to >=2000), accuracy peaks at 57.38% in the 1900-1999 bracket. These results show that modeling chess as a time-aware, sequential activity yields more human-accurate move prediction than position-only approaches, using a smaller model. Code, trained models, and complete training logs are publicly released.

</details>


### [89] [SearchAuditor: Auditing and Attributing Failures in Long-Horizon Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05212)
*Zhixiang Liang,Yifei Liu,Yidan Huang,Haozhe Zhao,Beichen Huang,Jiaqi Wang,Nan Duan,Qiong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SearchAuditBench基准和SearchAuditor审计框架，用于评估和提升LLM审计员对深度搜索智能体失败轨迹的定位、归因和修复能力，显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索智能体在执行长时序网页交互时极易出错，小推理错误会沿长噪声轨迹传播为错误的最终答案。诊断这类失败需要人工检查极长的执行轨迹，超出人类能力范围，因此亟需自动化审计工具来减轻人工负担。

Method: 构建SearchAuditBench：收集8个开源模型的1,243条失败轨迹（平均73.1条消息、65.1K token），覆盖5个深度搜索基准，由专家标注关键错误步骤、搜索特定根因和参考修复方案及评分标准。提出SearchAuditor：一个多视角审计框架，通过基于证据的裁决机制实现失败定位、归因和修复。

Result: 即使最强基线使用前沿模型GPT-5.5也只达到26.6%的端到端通过率；SearchAuditor在多个前沿模型上一致优于所有基线，达到32.3%的端到端通过率，且利用其修复方案恢复失败的运行可帮助智能体更好地从错误中恢复。

Conclusion: SearchAuditBench为评估LLM审计深度搜索失败的能力提供了标准化基准，SearchAuditor展示了通过证据驱动的多视角裁决策略有效提升审计性能，证明了自动化审计在减少人工负担方面的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Deep search agents tackle challenging questions through long-horizon web interactions, a process that is both complex and fragile: small reasoning errors may propagate through long, noisy trajectories into fluent but incorrect answers. Diagnosing such failures is difficult, requiring the manual inspection of extremely long execution traces, which could be beyond human capacity. We therefore introduce SearchAuditBench, a benchmark that evaluates whether LLM auditors can localize, attribute, and repair these failures, thereby reducing the human burden. SearchAuditBench comprises 1,243 failed trajectories, averaging 73.1 messages and 65.1K tokens, collected from eight open-weight models on five deep-search benchmarks, each expert-annotated with the critical error step, a search-specific root cause, and a reference repair with grading rubrics. We further propose SearchAuditor, a multi-perspective auditing framework that effectively localizes, attributes, and repairs search-agent failures through evidence-grounded adjudication. Experimental results show that even the strongest baseline, when powered by a frontier model like GPT-5.5, attains only a 26.6% end-to-end pass rate. In contrast, our SearchAuditor consistently outperforms all baselines across different frontier models, achieving an end-to-end pass rate of 32.3%, and resuming failed runs with its repairs enables agents to better recover from errors.

</details>


### [90] [PD-GS: Phoneme-Driven 3DGS for Audio-Driven Talking Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05218)
*Ao Fu,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PD-GS提出了一种基于音素驱动的3D高斯溅射说话人头像生成方法，通过时间对齐的音素标记与自适应语言融合模块，解决了唇部发音不准确和"漏嘴"伪影问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS说话人渲染虽然快速逼真，但唇部运动常被过度平滑，无法满足双唇闭合等硬性发音约束，产生"漏嘴"伪影。根本原因在于从连续声学嵌入回归离散发音事件时存在平均化偏差。

Method: PD-GS引入ASR和强制对齐流水线获取时间对齐的音素标记，核心组件Linguistic Fusion Module (LFM)通过学习的门控机制自适应融合连续音频上下文与离散音素嵌入，在发音关键片段增强音素引导，同时保持平滑的音频驱动动态。训练仅依赖单目视频的图像重建和唇部关键点监督。

Result: 在HDTF数据集上，PD-GS在唇部几何指标LMD上达到2.66，优于所有对比基线，并在具有挑战性的音素序列中显著减少闭合违规现象。

Conclusion: PD-GS通过离散音素标记与连续音频特征的自适应融合，生成了更符合语言学约束的神经头像，验证了在3DGS框架下融入显式语言先验的有效性。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables fast, photorealistic talking-head rendering, yet accurate lip articulation remains elusive: mouth motion is often over-smoothed and may violate hard articulatory constraints such as bilabial closures, producing the notorious ``leaky mouth'' artifact. A key difficulty is that brief, discrete articulatory events are inferred from a continuous acoustic embedding under a regression objective, which biases predictions toward averaged mouth configurations. While modern self-supervised speech encoders provide rich prosodic and phonetic cues, they do not provide an explicit, frame-aligned linguistic target that reliably disambiguates closure-level events. We propose \textbf{Phoneme-Driven Gaussian Splatting (PD-GS)}, which augments a 3DGS talker with time-aligned phoneme tokens obtained from an automatic ASR and forced-alignment pipeline. Our core component, the \textbf{Linguistic Fusion Module (LFM)}, adaptively fuses continuous audio context with discrete phoneme embeddings through a learned gate, allowing the model to preserve smooth audio-driven dynamics while strengthening phoneme guidance on articulation-critical segments. PD-GS is trained purely from monocular video using image reconstruction and lip landmark supervision. On HDTF, PD-GS achieves the best lip geometry among the compared baselines (LMD 2.66) and qualitatively reduces closure violations in challenging phoneme sequences, yielding more linguistically faithful neural avatars.

</details>


### [91] [When Privileged Guidance Misaligns: State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation for Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05219)
*Junzhuo Liu,Weiwei Li,Jun Ling,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出状态匹配路由与上下文自蒸馏（SMRC-SD），解决特权轨迹蒸馏中状态-参考不匹配问题。通过仅在学生当前执行状态与参考轨迹支持状态匹配时才进行蒸馏，并构造状态条件的教师上下文，在ALFWorld和WebShop上显著超越无条件全路径蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特权在线策略蒸馏为多轮智能体提供密集监督，但在交互环境中，学生的先前动作持续改变执行状态，导致其rollout可能到达参考轨迹未覆盖的状态，使参考成为当前状态不可靠的指导来源，造成状态-参考不匹配。

Method: 提出SMRC-SD框架：(1) 在每轮验证学生的当前执行状态是否与参考轨迹中的支持状态匹配，仅对匹配状态应用蒸馏，过滤掉参考缺乏局部兼容指导的轮次；(2) 对每个匹配状态，从成功轨迹构造状态条件的教师上下文，使监督基于实际到达的状态。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop上，SMRC-SD一致优于无条件成功全路径蒸馏。使用Qwen3-1.7B，ALFWorld任务成功率从0.746提升至0.865，WebShop从0.574提升至0.693。消融实验支持选择局部支持轮次和构造状态兼容教师上下文均对增益有贡献。

Conclusion: SMRC-SD通过显式判断何时以及如何使特权轨迹指导学生，有效缓解了状态-参考不匹配问题，验证了选择局部支持状态转换和状态条件教师上下文构建在特权蒸馏中的关键作用。

Abstract: Privileged on-policy distillation provides dense supervision for multi-turn agents by allowing a synchronized teacher to re-score the student's response at every turn with access to training-only references, such as successful trajectories. In interactive environments, however, the student's preceding actions continually change the execution state. As the student takes different actions or completes subgoals in a different order, its rollout may reach states not covered by the reference, making the reference an unreliable source of guidance for the state actually reached. Applying privileged distillation indiscriminately therefore creates state--reference mismatch. This mismatch motivates a central objective: providing privileged reference guidance that remains compatible with the student's current execution state. We introduce State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation (SMRC-SD), which explicitly determines when and how a privileged trajectory should guide an on-policy student. At each turn, SMRC-SD verifies whether the student's current execution state matches a supported state along the reference trajectory. Distillation is applied only at matched states, filtering out turns for which the reference lacks locally compatible guidance. For each matched state, SMRC-SD further constructs state-conditioned teacher context from the successful trajectory, grounding supervision in the state actually reached. Across ALFWorld and WebShop, SMRC-SD consistently outperforms unconditional successful full-path distillation. With Qwen3-1.7B, it improves task success from $0.746$ to $0.865$ on ALFWorld and from $0.574$ to $0.693$ on WebShop. Controlled routing and context ablations support both selecting locally supported turns and constructing state-compatible teacher context as contributors to these gains. Code is available at https://github.com/liujunzhuo/SMRC-SD.

</details>


### [92] [Small Foundation Models of Human Cognition and Behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05224)
*Nick Oh,Fernand Gobet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过在Psych-101数据集（160个实验、1070万次试次级选择）上训练14个不同规模（135M到14B参数）和4种架构族的大语言模型，探讨了认知微调语言模型的规模效应及其处理任务结构的方式。研究发现：分布内任务上规模几乎无关紧要，小模型即可达到天花板；但分布外泛化时规模梯度显著。通过提示通道剥离诊断和试次乱序置换，证明模型并非依赖选择历史这一统计捷径，而是真正处理了刺激和反馈信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在人类行为数据上微调后已成为通用认知代理，但两个关键问题悬而未决：一是所需模型规模到底多大，二是模型究竟是真正处理任务结构还是利用统计捷径。作者希望系统性地回答这两个问题。

Method: 在Psych-101数据集（160个认知心理学实验、1070万试次选择）上训练14个模型，跨越4个架构族（135M至14B参数）。分布内评估考察对已见参与者/实验的预测能力；分布外评估考察对新颖任务结构的泛化。使用两个诊断：(1) 逐步剥离四个提示通道（任务指令、实验刺激、结果反馈、选择历史）共27个实验；(2) 随机置换试次顺序，检验模型对试次间依赖结构的敏感度。

Result: 分布内性能对规模不敏感，所有模型落在一个狭窄区间内（如同受天花板约束），0.6B至1B参数即可匹敌70B基线。分布外性能随规模呈明显更陡的缩放梯度，更大模型在泛化到新颖任务结构时具有显著优势。剥离刺激内容与反馈会摧毁75.7%的已学习信息并使模型低于随机水平，表明选择历史并非唯一来源。试次置换显示：对独立试次任务模型不变，对试次顺序由先前响应决定的任务模型敏感。

Conclusion: 小规模认知微调模型可作为心理实验的噪声天花板估计器，具有成本效益优势；但其适用范围受训练数据所含范式的限制。模型通过学习真正处理刺激与反馈信息（而非仅依赖选择历史捷径）来完成任务。

Abstract: Large language models fine-tuned on human behavioural data have emerged as general-purpose cognitive proxies, but the scale this requires, and whether these models process task structure or exploit statistical shortcuts, remain open questions. We train fourteen models from 135M to 14B parameters across four architecture families on Psych-101, a dataset of 10.7 million trial-level choices from 160 experiments. In-distribution, scale barely matters. The models fall within a narrow band, as though against a ceiling, and 0.6B to 1B parameters suffice to match a 70B baseline on held-out participants. Out-of-distribution, that band opens into a markedly steeper scaling gradient, with larger models clearly advantaged in generalisation to novel task structure. To determine what information these models use, we run two diagnostics. We progressively strip four prompt channels -- task instructions, experimental stimuli, outcome feedback, and choice history -- across 27 experiments, and permute trial order. Masking the content of stimuli and feedback destroys 75.7% of learned information and pushes models below chance, demonstrating that choice history alone does not account for performance. Permutation reveals invariance on tasks with independent trials but sensitivity where trial order is determined by prior responses. Small cognitively fine-tuned models therefore show promise as noise ceiling estimators for psychological experiments, though their scope remains bounded by the paradigms seen in training.

</details>


### [93] [Project2Task: Graph-Guided Project-Level Planning for Autonomous Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05225)
*Huirui Xu,Runtao Xu,Shuo Ren,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Project2Task是一个图引导的项目级规划层，用于自主研究。它将项目brief表示为创新原子（innovation atoms）组织成有向谱系图，通过轻量级Bernoulli块模型目标在横向、纵向和混合组合分解中选择，生成具有依赖感知的任务契约（task contracts），包含目标、输入、预期产物、评估要求、边界约束、依赖和执行顺序。在十项项目brief约30个任务的基准上，Project2Task平均质量得分7.15，显著优于Brief Baseline（4.58）和Topic-only Setting（5.31）；与AutoResearchClaw集成后平均下游任务准确率从0.536提升至0.759。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究智能体能够执行文献搜索、假设提出、代码生成、实验运行和稿件撰写等任务，但研究项目并非简单的大型任务，而是需要多个有界任务推进的长周期议程（long-horizon agenda），这些任务具有不同但相关的目标、并行替代方案和依赖感知序列。现有单任务系统要么将项目当作一个超大任务处理，要么产生扁平模糊或重叠的任务集合，要么将任务边界和执行顺序留给人工协调，缺乏项目级别的规划能力。

Method: Project2Task包含三个核心步骤：（1）将项目brief中的候选贡献表示为创新原子（innovation atoms），并组织成有向谱系图（directed lineage graph）；（2）使用轻量级Bernoulli块模型目标在横向（horizontal）、纵向（vertical）和混合（hybrid）组合分解之间进行选择；（3）生成有界任务（bounded tasks），明确贡献归属，修复重叠和缺失的执行字段，并输出依赖感知的任务契约——契约指定目标、输入、预期产物、评估要求、边界约束、依赖关系和执行顺序。任务契约与具体的下游研究执行器无关，支持将任务输出整合为连贯的项目级结果。

Result: 在十项项目brief、约30个任务的基准上：（1）基于手稿的portfolio评估中，Project2Task平均质量得分7.15，优于Brief Baseline的4.58和Topic-only Setting的5.31；（2）将Project2Task的任务契约与AutoResearchClaw集成，平均下游任务准确率从0.536提升至0.759。结果表明明确的项目到任务规划能产生连贯、无冗余且可执行的研究任务组合。

Conclusion: Project2Task证明了显式的项目-任务规划层对于自主研究系统的重要性。通过将研究项目分解为具有明确贡献归属和依赖感知顺序的有界任务，能够显著提升任务组合的连贯性、避免冗余并保证可执行性，且任务契约的通用性使其可以与任何下游研究执行器集成。

Abstract: Research agents can increasingly search literature, propose hypotheses, generate code, run experiments, and draft manuscripts from a single topic. However, a research project is not merely a larger task: it is a long-horizon agenda that must be advanced through multiple bounded tasks with distinct but related objectives, parallel alternatives, and dependency-aware sequences. Existing single-task systems often treat the project as one oversized task, produce a flat set of vague or overlapping tasks, or leave task boundaries and execution order to manual coordination. We introduce Project2Task, a graph-guided project-level planning layer for autonomous research. Given a project brief, it represents candidate contributions as innovation atoms and organizes them in a directed lineage graph. A lightweight Bernoulli block-model objective selects among horizontal, vertical, and hybrid portfolio decompositions. Project2Task then generates bounded tasks with explicit contribution ownership, repairs overlaps and missing execution fields, and emits dependency-aware task contracts that specify objectives, inputs, expected artifacts, evaluation requirements, boundary constraints, dependencies, and execution order. The contracts are independent of any particular downstream research executor and support integration of task outputs into a coherent project-level result. On a benchmark of ten project briefs yielding roughly 30 tasks, manuscript-based portfolio evaluation gives Project2Task an average quality score of 7.15, compared with 4.58 for the Brief Baseline and 5.31 for the Topic-only Setting. Integrating its contracts with AutoResearchClaw increases average downstream task accuracy from 0.536 to 0.759. These results demonstrate the value of explicit project-to-task planning for producing coherent, non-redundant, and executable research-task portfolios.

</details>


### [94] [TriQua: Reconciling Granularity and Context in Factuality Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05228)
*Jin Liu,Steffen Thoma,Achim Rettinger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了TriQua框架，通过区分简单三元组和带上下文限定符的超关系事实来灵活建模事实，解决了LLM事实性评估中"先分解后验证"范式的原子性与上下文保留之间的权衡问题，并配套提出TriQuaScore评分方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的"先分解后验证"范式在LLM事实性评估中面临根本性权衡：原子事实（每个句子表达单一信息单元）往往丢失关键上下文，而更宽泛的表述又缺乏精确评估所需的粒度。需要一种能够根据事实复杂度灵活建模的方案。

Method: TriQua框架采用适应性结构：简单声明提取为标准三元组（subject-predicate-object），复杂声明通过附加辅助上下文限定符（qualifiers）表示为超关系事实（hyperrelational facts）。验证过程直接在具体三元组和限定符上标注具体错误，提供细粒度的可解释性。同时提出TriQuaScore对结构化事实单元的事实性进行量化评分。

Result: 实验表明TriQuaScore与人工标注的事实性分数高度一致；TriQua在分解质量上表现稳健，且在基于证据的事实验证任务中优于现有基于分解的框架。

Conclusion: TriQua通过灵活的事实结构建模有效平衡了原子性与上下文保留之间的矛盾，在保持精确评估粒度的同时避免了关键上下文丢失，并提供了细粒度、可解释的错误标注能力。

Abstract: The "decompose-then-verify" paradigm for LLM factuality evaluation faces a fundamental trade-off: atomic facts, i.e., one sentence conveying one unit of information, often omit essential context, while broader statements lack the granularity needed for precise assessment. To address this, we introduce TriQua, a framework that flexibly models facts based on their complexity. Simple claims are extracted as standard triples, while complex claims are represented as hyperrelational facts by attaching auxiliary contextual qualifiers. This adaptive structure preserves the necessary context for accurate retrieval and verification without sacrificing atomicity. Furthermore, TriQua's verification process directly annotates concrete errors within specific triples and qualifiers, providing fine-grained explainability for error detection. Alongside the framework, we propose TriQuaScore to quantify the factuality of these structured fact units. Empirical evaluations show that TriQuaScore strongly aligns with human annotated factuality scores, TriQua achieves robust decomposition quality, and outperforms existing decomposition-based frameworks in evidence-based fact verification.

</details>


### [95] [Coherence-Oriented Dream Scene Visualisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05233)
*Azra Açıl,Simon Colton*

Main category: cs.AI
